D5 read-owner checkpoint clarification (2026-10-08): readNativeAnalysisState accepts an optional sixth trusted scalar-record observer (BASELINE_READ_SQL_OWNERSHIP_ONLY), independent of its fifth cumulative READ observer. TOKEN_ROW adds only the current six-column raw row and previously accepted three-field token tuples; TOKEN_ACCEPTED additionally owns the current parsed digest but does not count provider/snapshot strings twice while they alias that raw row.
